# Error analysis

Analysis of saved predictions only. Validation and test are reported separately.

All scoring uses `huasr.text` and `huasr.metrics` with accents retained. WER and CER are total edits divided by total reference words and characters, respectively. Empty-reference rows, if any, are listed separately rather than included in rates.

Long means at least 12 Unicode letters in the normalized reference token. The word-length error rate is (substitutions + deletions) / reference tokens of that length; insertions do not belong to a reference length bin.

Accent-only means distinct aligned words become identical after the project's accent stripping. After excluding those, suffix-only means two alphabetic words share an exact initial prefix of at least four letters and at least half the shorter word, with no more than five letters remaining on either side. Empty tails are allowed. This is an ending-only surface heuristic, not a morphological analysis.

An overlong hypothesis is at least 1.5 times the reference length and has at least five extra normalized words. A loop candidate also repeats the same 1–8-word phrase at least three times adjacently and at least twice more than the longest reference run. These flags suggest repetition, but are not judged by listening.
